## 1.0.0 (2026-09-23)

### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* P2P event names changed; requires all peers to update simultaneously.
* all import paths under @axe/ have changed
* application now runs in zoneless mode
* components now use signal services instead of EventSystem.trigger

### ✨ Features

* add eslint-plugin-unused-imports to manage unused imports in the project ([92a0a1b](https://github.com/hibohiboo/udonarium_axe/commit/92a0a1b2c3b74f02cbb2410a83715b9bec6ed3f4))
